Coding - Chapter 218

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the coding structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 339. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 91 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on coding structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 339. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 91. QED.
